- **langgraph-graphrecursionerror-how-to-r-77bd788ce8f11417**: LangGraph raises langgraph.errors.GraphRecursionError when a graph run exceeds recursion_limit supersteps (default 25). Covers how to raise the limit in the run config for legitimately long agent loops, and how to debug real cycles instead of raising it: conditional-edge exit conditions, tracing repeated nodes, per-run limits, and catching the error to return partial results. Use when graph.invoke or graph.stream fails with GraphRecursionError or max supersteps reached. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/langgraph-graphrecursionerror-how-to-r-77bd788ce8f11417))
- **langgraph-graphrecursionerror-recursio-19ff5ebe57b6bff1**: Fixes LangGraph GraphRecursionError when the graph hits the default 25-step recursion limit. Use when an agent loop never terminates, when two nodes bounce control back and forth, or when a router keeps sending work to the same node. Not for must-write errors, for invalid checkpoint updates, or for genuinely long workflows. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/langgraph-graphrecursionerror-recursio-19ff5ebe57b6bff1))
- **langgraph-graphrecursionerror-recursio-99a44d4b92d1a2f0**: Fixes LangGraph GraphRecursionError when a graph exceeds the default 25-step recursion limit. Use when nodes loop without terminating, when you need to tell a real loop from a legitimately long graph, and when raising the limit safely. Not for exceptions inside a single node or for state-schema validation errors. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/langgraph-graphrecursionerror-recursio-99a44d4b92d1a2f0))
